In real-world experiments on two different platforms, the adaptive policy outperforms non-adaptive learning-based baselines by up to 58% in position mean absolute error while approaching the tracking accuracy of a platform-specific tuned controller.

</details>


### [289] [Guided Action Flow: Q-Guided Inference for Flow-Matching Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02092)
*Liuhaichen Yang,Zhuang Jiang,Chenchao Sheng,Zezhi Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Guided Action Flow框架，通过训练动作块评价器（critic）对冻结的流匹配视觉-语言-动作策略（SmolVLA）进行推理时引导，无需重新训练基础策略。在LIBERO任务上，单任务评价器将成功率从68.0%提升至82.0%，多家族评价器将验证成功率从46.0%提升至56.0%，但泛化能力和不确定性感知引导仍是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配VLA策略通过迭代传输过程生成机器人动作块，这为测试时引导提供了机会。研究者希望在不重训基础策略的前提下，利用已学习的评价器在推理时引导预训练策略，从而提升性能。

Method: 保持预训练SmolVLA策略冻结，训练一个动作块评价器（critic），该评价器可从真实成功与失败回放中学习，能够利用冻结SmolVLA语言路径中的任务描述特征，在采样过程中通过动作梯度进行引导。

Result: 单任务评价器：成功率从68.0%提升至82.0%（种子窗口1），从82.0%提升至86.0%（种子窗口2）。多家族任务描述评价器：验证成功率从46.0%提升至56.0%；锁定的留出测试成功率从65.0%提升至67.5%。

Conclusion: Q引导推理对于冻结的流匹配VLA策略是可行的，但评价器的泛化能力和不确定性感知引导仍是核心瓶颈。

Abstract: Flow-matching vision-language-action policies generate robot action chunks through an iterative transport process, creating an opportunity for test-time guidance without retraining the base policy. We study this opportunity in Guided Action Flow, an inference-time framework that keeps a pretrained SmolVLA policy frozen and uses a learned action-chunk critic to guide its reverse-time flow sampler. The critic is trained from real success and failure rollouts, can condition on task-description features from the frozen SmolVLA language pathway, and is used only through action gradients during sampling. We evaluate the approach on LIBERO manipulation tasks. A single-task critic improves success from 68.0% to 82.0% on one seed window and from 82.0% to 86.0% on another. A multi-family task-description critic improves validation success from 46.0% to 56.0%, while the locked held-out test gain is positive but modest, from 65.0% to 67.5%. These results support the feasibility of Q-guided inference for frozen flow-matching VLA policies, while showing that critic generalization and uncertainty-aware guidance remain the central bottlenecks.

</details>


### [290] [Choreographing the Way of Water: A Computational Framework for Aquatic Robotic Art](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02174)
*Aswin Ramachandran,Christopher Golling,Sebastian Burmester,Noa Sendlhofer,Jan Kamm,Ruiheng Jiang,Raffaello D'Andrea*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Way of Water系统，一种将自主水面船队作为分布式编舞平台的网络物理架构。核心贡献是Way of Water Studio——一个基于浏览器的DAW式可视化时间线创作范式，集成了序列凸规划轨迹生成和模型预测控制抗干扰技术，使非编程艺术家也能编排水上机器人舞蹈。系统在苏黎世湖（18艘船）和威尼斯双年展（8艘船）得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放水域的机器人编舞受非线性流体动力学制约，环境干扰和系统非线性带来显著挑战；现有方法局限于水面2D像素范式，缺乏面向非编程艺术家的3D体积域水上编舞工具。

Method: 提出Way of Water框架：①3D体积域表达：层流喷嘴和多区域灯光将船队从2D水面扩展到3D空间；②Way of Water Studio：浏览器端时间线合成创作范式，类似DAW乐器，集成SCP（序列凸规划）轨迹生成和MPC（模型预测控制）抗干扰；③全栈系统：定制全向底盘、水环境状态估计与控制栈、LTE/MQTT通信链路及RTK-GPS时间同步。

Result: 在两个不同场景完成验证：苏黎世湖18艘船演绎《天鹅湖》，威尼斯Forte Marghera的Time Space Existence 2025双年展8艘船演示。

Conclusion: 该项工作为流体机器人蜂群的设计与部署建立了基础性参考，验证了从2D水面到3D体积域的水上编舞可行性，并降低了非编程艺术家使用高性能水上机器人的门槛。

Abstract: Robotic choreography in open water is governed by nonlinear fluid dynamics, which impose significant challenges due to environmental disturbances and nonlinear system dynamics. This paper presents the cyber-physical architecture of Way of Water, a vertically integrated framework that orchestrates a fleet of autonomous surface vessels as a distributed choreographic platform. Moving beyond the surface-pixel paradigm, these vessels use laminar nozzles and multi-zone lighting to extend their expressive range from the 2D water plane into the 3D volumetric domain. Our primary contribution is the Way of Water Studio, a browser-based, timeline-compositing authoring paradigm that treats the fleet as a DAW-like instrument for music-responsive choreography. The Studio encapsulates Sequential Convex Programming for trajectory generation and Model Predictive Control for disturbance rejection presented through a visual timeline, broadening access to high-performance aquatic robotics for non-programmer artists. Grounding the Studio is the full cyber-physical stack: a custom holonomic chassis, a state-estimation and control stack tuned for the aquatic domain, and an LTE/MQTT fleet link with RTK-GPS time synchronization. We report on the system's validation across two distinct deployments: an 18-vessel Swan Lake interpretation at Lake Zurich and an 8-vessel Time Space Existence 2025 Venice Biennale demonstration at Forte Marghera, establishing a foundational reference for the design and deployment of fluidic robotic swarms.

</details>


### [291] [Bridge-WA: Predicting Where and How the World Changes for Robotic Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02195)
*Yongjie Bai,Hanting Wang,Mingtong Dai,Qijun Zhong,Yang Liu,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Bridge-WA提出一种轻量级世界-动作框架，通过蒸馏冻结的未来变化教师模型得到三种紧凑先验（未来令牌、变化图、运动流图），在推理时无需密集未来图像生成，即可提升视觉-语言-动作模型在操作任务中的泛化性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界-动作模型依赖大规模生成式世界模型或密集未来展开，计算成本高且过度建模与动作控制弱相关的视觉细节，导致泛化能力受限。

Method: 将冻结的未来变化教师模型蒸馏为三种紧凑先验（未来令牌、变化图、运动流图），通过多源注意力记忆和时空偏置将这些先验条件化到动作Transformer中，推理时移除教师模型。

Result: 在VLABench、RoboTwin2.0、LIBERO-Plus及真实机器人评估中，Bridge-WA在任务成功率、进展和鲁棒性方面均取得提升，尤其在分布外视觉偏移下增益明显。

Conclusion: 通过聚焦动作生成于场景变化的位置和方式，Bridge-WA有效抑制背景、光照、干扰物等无关外观因素，在不依赖部署时密集未来图像生成的情况下实现更好的泛化。

Abstract: General-purpose vision-language-action models benefit from large vision-language priors, but effective manipulation also requires anticipating action-relevant scene changes. Existing world-action models often rely on large generative world models or dense future rollouts, which are expensive and spend capacity on visual details weakly coupled to control. We present Bridge-WA, a lightweight world-action framework that distills a frozen future-change teacher into three compact priors: future tokens for intended outcomes, change maps for intervention support, and motion-flow maps for local transition direction. A WorldBridge conditions the action transformer on these priors through multi-source attention memories and spatial-temporal biases, while the teacher model is removed at inference. Across VLABench, RoboTwin2.0, LIBERO-Plus and real-robot evaluations, Bridge-WA improves task success, progress, and robustness, with particularly clear gains under out-of-distribution visual shifts. By focusing action generation on where and how the scene will change, Bridge-WA suppresses nuisance appearance factors such as background, lighting, and distractors, leading to better generalization without deployment-time dense future-image generation. Code and visualizations are available at: https://hcplab-sysu.github.io/BRIDGE-WA .

</details>


### [292] [Actuator Reality Shaping for Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02205)
*Satoshi Yamamori,Koji Ishihara,Kentaro Minamikawa,Kiyoharu Ohomori,Taiyo Yazaki,Norikazu Sugimoto,Jun Morimoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"执行器现实塑造"(Actuator Reality Shaping)范式，通过为物理执行器配备二自由度前馈-反馈控制器，使其闭环行为匹配仿真中使用的理想二阶参考动力学，从而实现强化学习策略从仿真到真实硬件的零样本迁移，无需提高仿真保真度或任务级微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习中的仿真到现实迁移常受限于理想执行器动力学与物理电机非线性、硬件相关行为之间的差异，现有方法通过提高仿真保真度来弥合这一差距，但本文提出相反的思路：不修改仿真器，而是改造物理执行器的行为。

Method: 为每个关节配备二自由度前馈-反馈控制器，将参考响应塑形与鲁棒镇定解耦，使物理执行器的闭环行为匹配仿真中使用的理想二阶参考动力学，从而为标准化的强化学习策略接口提供统一的执行器行为。

Result: 在单关节高减速比伺服电机（带外部负载）和7自由度机械臂到达任务中，该方法相比标准伺服控制和代表性的"真实到仿真再到真实"基线，显著降低了仿真到现实的跟踪误差，并提升了零样本任务性能。此外，在轮腿机器人斜坡行驶和仿人机器人行走任务中也验证了零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: 执行器现实塑造可作为跨不同硬件平台的机器人学习的可复用接口，无需任务级微调或学习执行器模型即可实现零样本仿真到现实迁移。

Abstract: Sim-to-real transfer in robot learning is often limited by discrepancies between the ideal actuator dynamics assumed during policy training and the nonlinear, hardware-dependent behavior of physical motors. While conventional approaches attempt to bridge this gap by increasing simulator fidelity through system identification, domain randomization, or learned actuator models, we introduce an alternative paradigm: actuator reality shaping. Instead of modifying the simulator to match the real world, our method shapes the closed-loop behavior of physical actuators to match the idealized second-order reference dynamics used in simulation. By equipping each joint with a two-degree-of-freedom feedforward--feedback controller, we decouple reference-response shaping from robust stabilization, thereby providing a standardized actuator interface for reinforcement learning policies. As a result, policies trained only with the prescribed reference model can be deployed zero-shot on real hardware without task-level fine-tuning or learned actuator models. We validate the approach on a single-joint high-gear-ratio servo under external loads and a 7-DOF robotic arm reaching task, where actuator reality shaping substantially reduces sim-to-real tracking error and improves zero-shot task performance compared with standard servo-control and representative real-to-sim-to-real baselines. We further demonstrate zero-shot transfer on a wheeled-legged robot driving over a slope and a humanoid robot walking, suggesting that actuator reality shaping can serve as a reusable interface for robot learning across diverse hardware platforms.

</details>


### [293] [CoFL-S: Spatially Queryable Sector Flow Fields for Local Language-Conditioned Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02222)
*Haokun Liu,Zhaoqi Ma,Yicheng Chen,Wentao Zhang,Masaki Kitagawa,Zicen Xiong,Jinjie Li,Moju Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CoFL-S，一种低层级视觉-语言-动作框架，通过预测由语言条件化的流场来生成连续轨迹，在连续时间Habitat基准测试中优于动作token和动作chunk基线，并在零样本真实世界部署中展现了优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言导航领域虽然在高层级指令推理、记忆、全局地图构建和指令分解方面取得了进展，但低层级动作表示仍未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出CoFL-S框架，预测语言条件化的流场，将VLN-CE片段转换为帧级局部监督（包含对齐的子指令、匹配的动作、轨迹和稠密流场目标），并引入连续时间Habitat基准以隔离低层级动作接口。

Result: 在匹配编码器和训练设置下，CoFL-S在不同规划器频率下均优于动作token和动作chunk基线；零样本真实世界闭环部署也证明了其优势。

Conclusion: CoFL-S作为一种低层级动作表示方法，通过流场预测和连续轨迹生成，在仿真和真实世界部署中均表现出优于现有方法的导航性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation has increasingly emphasized high-level instruction reasoning, memory, global map construction, and instruction decomposition, while the low-level action representation remains comparatively underexplored. We propose CoFL-S, a low-level vision-language-action framework that predicts a language-conditioned flow field over the robot's local visible sector and generates continuous trajectories by rolling out the predicted field. To train this low-level representation, we convert each VLN-CE episode, originally a whole-episode instruction paired with an action sequence, into frame-level local supervision with aligned sub-instructions and matched action, trajectory, and dense flow-field targets. For evaluation, we introduce a continuous-time Habitat benchmark that isolates low-level action interfaces from instruction decomposition and executes all methods through a shared velocity-command controller, enabling decomposition-independent closed-loop comparison across different planner frequencies rather than fixed discrete forward-and-turn transitions in VLN-CE. Under matched encoders and training settings, CoFL-S consistently outperforms action-token and action-chunk baselines across planner frequencies in the continuous-time Habitat benchmark, and zero-shot real-world closed-loop deployment further shows its advantage over both baselines beyond simulation.

</details>


### [294] [NEUROSYMLAND: Neuro-Symbolic Landing-Site Assessment for Robust and Edge-Deployable UAV Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02277)
*Weixian Qian,Tianyi Yang,Sebastian Schroder,Yao Deng,Jiaohong Yao,Xiao Cheng,Richard Han,Xi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NEUROSYMLAND是一个神经符号着陆点评估系统，结合轻量级感知与显式符号安全推理，通过概率语义场景图和符号约束实现边缘设备上的鲁棒、可解释着陆评估，在72个模拟场景中达到61次成功评估，优于四种基线（37-57次），并通过100次硬件在环实验验证了边缘资源受限下的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉学习方法在非结构化环境中面对地形变化时性能下降，且缺乏安全决策的可解释性，亟需一种既鲁棒又透明的着陆点评估系统。

Method: 构建概率语义场景图（PSSG），利用符号约束（地形平坦度、障碍物间距、空间一致性）在感知不确定性下进行结构化推理，系统部署于边缘计算平台。

Result: 72个模拟场景中61次成功评估（优于基线37-57次）；100次硬件在环试验完成端到端延迟、分阶段执行时间、CPU/GPU利用率、内存占用及功耗的系统级指标分析。

Conclusion: 符号推理仅占端到端延迟的一小部分，主要计算开销来自感知和PSSG构建；系统在边缘受限硬件上可行，具备鲁棒性和可解释性，且全部代码、数据集、提示和符号规则已开源。

Abstract: Safe landing-site assessment in unstructured environments remains a key challenge for autonomous UAV deployment, as vision-only learning approaches often degrade under terrain variability and provide limited transparency in safety decisions. We present NEUROSYMLAND, a neuro-symbolic landing-site assessment system that integrates lightweight perception with explicit safety reasoning. The framework constructs a probabilistic semantic scene graph from onboard visual input and evaluates candidate landing regions using symbolic constraints capturing terrain flatness, obstacle clearance, and spatial consistency, enabling structured reasoning under perceptual uncertainty while maintaining edge-feasible execution. Across 72 simulated landing scenarios spanning diverse terrains, NEUROSYMLAND achieves 61 successful assessments, outperforming four competitive baselines (37-57 successes). To evaluate deployability, we further conduct 100 hardware-in-the-loop trials with randomized initial poses, profiling end-to-end latency, stage-wise execution time, and system-level metrics including CPU/GPU utilization, memory footprint, and power consumption. Results demonstrate improved robustness and interpretability with bounded edge-resource usage. Profiling shows that symbolic reasoning contributes only a small fraction of end-to-end latency, while the main computational cost arises from perception and PSSG construction. These results demonstrate the feasibility of deploying the landing-site assessment stack on edge-constrained UAV hardware, and all source code, datasets, prompts, and symbolic rule refinement examples are released in an open-source repository

</details>


### [295] [Real-Time Visual Intelligence on Low-Cost UAVs: A Modular Approach for Tracking, Scanning, and Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02298)
*Andrei-Marian Ungureanu,Stelian Spînu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文基于DJI Tello无人机平台，开发了一套集成人脸检测、人脸识别和单目深度估计的智能无人机个人助理系统，采用模块化架构、Web界面控制和轻量级神经网络，验证了低成本硬件上部署先进AI技术的可行性，适用于军事、救援和 surveillance 等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有商业解决方案成本高、不开放，缺乏低成本、易获取的智能无人机个人助理系统。作者希望利用开源技术和廉价硬件，将先进AI技术集成到无人机平台，使其成为个人助理。

Method: 基于DJI Tello无人机平台，采用模块化架构集成三种AI功能：人脸检测、人脸识别和单目深度估计。通过Web界面控制无人机和实时视频监控，Python服务器处理视觉数据，使用针对嵌入式系统优化的轻量级神经网络模型进行推理。

Result: 系统在真实场景中表现鲁棒，实现了人员跟踪、室内扫描和基于虚拟传感器的自主线跟随等功能，验证了在受限硬件上部署先进AI技术的可行性。

Conclusion: 该项目验证了将先进AI技术应用于实时机器人系统的可行性，证明了在低成本、受限硬件上部署AI的可行性，为未来自主无人机在军事、救援和监视等任务中的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Autonomous drones are rapidly transforming modern warfare and civil applications alike. This paper presents the development of an integrated intelligent drone system designed to serve as a personal assistant. Leveraging the DJI Tello drone platform, we implemented a modular architecture that integrates three core artificial intelligence functionalities: facial detection, facial recognition, and depth estimation from monocular vision. A web-based interface enables seamless drone control and real-time video monitoring, while a Python-based server processes visual data and executes inference pipelines using lightweight neural models optimized for embedded systems. Unlike existing commercial solutions, this system emphasizes accessibility, low-cost hardware, and open-source technologies. The system demonstrates robust performance in real-world conditions, including person tracking, indoor scanning, and autonomous line following using virtual sensors. This project validates the applicability of advanced AI techniques in real-time robotic systems and illustrates the feasibility of deploying them on constrained hardware, providing a foundation for future research in autonomous UAVs for military, rescue, and surveillance missions.

</details>


### [296] [The Moving Eye: Enhancing VLA Spatial Generalization via Hybrid Dynamic Data Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02322)
*Jincheng Tang,Yilong Zhu,Zhengyuan Xie,Jiang-Jiang Liu,Jiaxing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动的混合视角策略（连续相机运动+多样静态视角），用于增强VLA模型的空间泛化能力，有效减少捷径学习带来的虚假相关性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在机器人操作任务中空间泛化能力薄弱，简单增加视角数量无法解决模型因捷径学习而依赖虚假相关性的问题。

Method: 采用双臂机器人设置，一臂执行操作，另一臂作为移动环境相机。系统评估三种数据分布模式（固定视角、多固定视角、移动视角），提出混合策略：连续相机运动与多样静态视角相结合。

Result: 混合数据策略显著优于仅增加静态视角的方法，有效减少虚假相关性，使VLA模型能泛化到未见过的相机姿态和物体配置。所有评估模型（ACT、Diffusion、Pi0、Gr00t）均受益于该策略。

Conclusion: VLA模型对捷径学习的易感性和空间泛化困难是跨架构的普遍特征，混合数据策略是提升各类VLA模型空间泛化能力的有效通用方法。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown remarkable promise in generalized robotic manipulation. However, their spatial generalization remains fragile. We argue that simply increasing the number of viewpoints is insufficient. Models often fall into the trap of Shortcut Learning, latching onto spurious correlations (e.g., fixed relative poses between objects or between the camera and robot base) rather than learning true spatial relationships. In this work, we propose a data-centric solution to enhance VLA spatial generalization. We utilize a dual-arm setup where one arm performs manipulation while the other serves as a mobile environmental camera. We systematically evaluate three data distribution patterns: Fixed, Multi-Fixed, and Moving Views. Our findings reveal that a hybrid strategy, combining continuous camera motion with diverse static viewpoints, yields the best performance by substantially reducing spurious correlations while maintaining training stability. Our experiments demonstrate that this strategy mitigates spurious correlations, enabling VLAs to generalize to unseen camera poses and object configurations where simply adding more static viewpoints fails. Crucially, we reveal that the susceptibility to shortcut learning and the struggle with spatial generalization are universal characteristics shared across diverse architectures. Consequently, all evaluated models (ACT, Diffusion, and VLA models including Pi0 and Gr00t) benefit significantly from our mixed data strategy.

</details>


### [297] [HEFT: Heavy-Payload Full-size Humanoid Teleoperation with Privileged Motion Guidance and Windowed Payload Curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02332)
*Chenxin Liu,Qingzhou Lu,Guangxiao Yang,Xuanyang Shi,Chenghan Yang,Yanjiang Guo,Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HEFT是一个面向全尺寸重型负载人形机器人的遥操作框架，通过特权运动引导（PMG）和窗口化负载课程（WPC）解决VR追踪噪声、漂移和重定位误差问题，在175cm、65kg的L7人形机器人上实现了高达24kg负载下的运动跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动跟踪与遥操作框架大多在紧凑平台上验证，缺乏对全尺寸人形机器人在真实负载交互下的探索。全尺寸人形机器人因惯性大、平衡裕度紧，对VR追踪器的噪声、漂移和重定位误差极为敏感，同时其负载潜力未被充分利用。

Method: HEFT包含两个核心组件：1) 特权运动引导（PMG），从可部署的含噪VR参考中学习物理上合理的重建参考；2) 窗口化负载课程（WPC），结合专家引导的负载上限，逐步训练机器人掌握鲁棒的重负载跟踪能力。

Result: 在175cm、65kg的全尺寸人形机器人L7上部署，实现了包括转身、前进/后退 locomotion、深蹲等动作的跟踪，最大负载达到24kg。

Conclusion: HEFT有效解决了全尺寸人形机器人在真实负载下的遥操作挑战，验证了从含噪VR参考中学习鲁棒运动跟踪的可行性，为大规模人形技能获取提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: General motion tracking and teleoperation offer a promising path to scalable humanoid skill acquisition, yet most existing frameworks are validated on compact platforms or without real payload interaction, leaving full-size humanoids with real payloads largely unexplored. Scaling to full-size humanoids introduces two compounding challenges: their larger inertia and tighter balance margins make tracking highly sensitive to noise, drift, and retargeting errors from commodity VR trackers, while their payload potential remains largely underutilized. We present HEFT, a heavy-payload full-size humanoid teleoperation framework that addresses both challenges. HEFT learns from deployable noisy VR references with physically plausible reconstructed references through Privileged Motion Guidance (PMG), and uses a Windowed Payload Curriculum (WPC) with expert-guided payload caps to acquire robust heavy-payload tracking. We deploy HEFT on L7, a 175cm, 65kg humanoid. The robot tracks motions including turns, forward/backward locomotion, and squats under payloads up to 24kg.

</details>


### [298] [ACID: Action Consistency via Inverse Dynamics for Planning with World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02403)
*Gawon Seo,Dongwon Kim,Suha Kwak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ACID提出了一种基于循环动作一致性（cycle action consistency）的决策时规划框架，通过逆动力学模型检查预测轨迹中每步动作的可实现性，并引入尺度不变自适应权重将其融入规划成本，在多种世界模型和任务中一致提升了规划效果，并以更少计算量达到基线精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准决策时规划仅通过预测终止状态与目标的接近程度来评估候选动作序列，忽略了中间状态转移的可实现性，导致预测轨迹看起来合理但在实际环境中 rollout 时会偏离。

Method: 提出ACID框架，引入循环动作一致性（cycle action consistency）：通过逆动力学模型从预测的转移中反向推断动作，应与前向条件化的动作一致；将该残差通过尺度不变自适应权重融入规划成本函数。

Result: 在4种动作条件世界模型和6个任务（刚体/可变形操作、关节控制、视觉导航）上，ACID一致提升了规划性能，并以显著更少的规划计算量达到与基线相当的精度。

Conclusion: 循环动作一致性是一种有效的正则化手段，能够在不增加推理时计算负担的前提下，提升决策时规划的鲁棒性和效率。

Abstract: Decision-time planning with action-conditioned world models has become a popular paradigm for embodied control. However, the standard planning cost judges a candidate solely by how close its predicted terminal state lies to the goal, leaving the realizability of the intermediate transitions unchecked -- a predicted trajectory can look convincing while the environment rollout drifts away from it. In this paper, we propose ACID, a decision-time planning framework that introduces cycle action consistency: the action inferred backward from a predicted transition by an inverse dynamics model should recover the one that was conditioned on. We fold this per-step residual into the planning cost via a scale-invariant adaptive weight. Across four action-conditioned world models and six tasks spanning rigid and deformable manipulation, articulated control, and visual navigation, ACID consistently improves planning and matches the baseline's accuracy with substantially less planning compute.

</details>


### [299] [LIME: Learning Intent-aware Camera Motion from Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02417)
*Boyang Sun,Jiajie Li,Yung-Hsu Yang,Chenyangguang Zhang,Tim Engelbracht,Sunghwan Hong,Cesar Cadena,Marc Pollefeys,Hermann Blum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LIME模型，利用第一人称视频中的多意图相机运动监督，将自然语言意图映射为相对SE(3)目标相机位姿，实现语言条件驱动的主动感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言导航和操作策略已能处理指令驱动的基座运动和操作，但语言条件触发的相机运动（作为一等动作）尚未被充分探索。机器人需自主移动相机以检查物体、揭露遮挡区域或响应用户意图，因此有必要建模这一任务。

Method: 从第一人称视频中挖掘多意图相机运动监督数据，将合理的意图和观测增益描述与相对SE(3)目标位姿配对。提出LIME模型，结合自回归观测增益输出与连续流匹配位姿头，联合预测下一视图的观测增益并表征多假设目标位姿。

Result: 实验和下游机器人任务表明，LIME能从被动人类视频中学习主动选择相机位姿，将普通第一人称记录转化为意图感知的主动感知监督信号。

Conclusion: LIME成功将语言条件相机运动生成作为一等动作建模，证明了从被动视频中学习意图驱动的主动相机控制是可行的，为具身智能体的主动感知提供了新范式。

Abstract: Autonomous robots often need to move their camera before they can act: to inspect an object, reveal an occluded region, or obtain a view that responds to a user's intent. While vision-language navigation translates instructions to base motion and vision-language-action policies map instructions to manipulation actions, language-conditioned camera motion remains comparatively underexplored as a first-class action. We formulate language-conditioned camera motion generation: given a current RGB observation and a free-form natural-language intent, predict a relative target camera pose for the next observation. This task is inherently non-trivial: viewpoint changes are driven by latent perceptual intentions, and a valid motion may operate at different semantic granularity, from entering a room to looking around a corner, inspecting a visible object, or revealing an occluded detail. To model this structure, we mine multi-intention camera-motion supervision from egocentric video, pairing plausible intents and observation-gain descriptions with relative SE(3) target poses. We propose LIME, a vision-language camera-motion generator that combines an auto-regressive observation-gain output with a continuous flow-matching pose head. This design lets the model jointly predict what the next view should reveal while representing multi-hypothesis target views. Across experiments and downstream robotic tasks, we show that LIME can learn to actively choose camera poses from passive human video, turning ordinary egocentric recordings into supervision for intent-aware active perception.

</details>


### [300] [WorldSample: Closed-loop Real-robot RL with World Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02431)
*Yuquan Xue,Le Xu,Zeyi Liu,Zhenyu Wu,Zhengyi Gu,Xinyang Song,Bofang Jia,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WorldSample是一个用于真实机器人强化学习的物理基础数据增强框架，通过物理 rollout、世界模型生成和策略改进之间的真实-合成闭环，结合策略节奏学习（PPL）来平衡有用增强与值过估计，在接触密集和精确操作任务中实现了28%的成功率提升和59%的训练步骤减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人强化学习面临交互成本高的问题，每次物理 rollout 代价昂贵且仅反映单一动作-结果路径，而模仿学习受限于演示覆盖范围，RL虽能通过试错超越演示覆盖但实际部署仍受高交互成本约束。

Method: 提出WorldSample框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 基于真实rollout后训练世界模型生成高保真合成转移；2) 策略节奏学习(PPL)通过样本选择和调度来调节训练过程，平衡有用增强与值过估计，减轻幻觉引入的噪声；3) 形成真实rollout、世界模型生成和策略改进之间的真实-合成闭环。

Result: 在涉及接触密集和精确操作的机器人操作任务上，相较于基线方法，WorldSample将策略成功率提升28%，同时减少59%的训练步骤。世界模型视觉保真度在PSNR上提升19.4dB，SSIM上提升0.47，优于仅基于演示的后训练方法。

Conclusion: WorldSample通过真实-合成闭环有效降低了真实机器人RL的交互成本，同时提升了策略性能和数据效率，验证了该框架对策略和世界模型性能的双重提升效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) can overcome the demonstration-coverage limitation of imitation learning (IL) by allowing robots to improve through trial-and-error interaction beyond the states observed in demonstrations. However, deploying RL on real robots remains constrained by high interaction costs, since each physical rollout is costly and reflects only one realized action-outcome path. To address this challenge, we propose WorldSample, a physically grounded data augmentation framework for real-robot RL that closes a real-synthetic loop between physical rollouts, world-model generation, and policy improvement. Grounded on real rollouts, WorldSample generates high-fidelity synthetic transitions through a post-trained world model, which greatly lowers the visual hallucination. Specifically, rather than simply using these transitions as real-world experience, WorldSample introduces Policy-Paced Learning (PPL) to regulate the training process through sample selection and scheduling, balancing useful augmentation against value overestimation and mitigating the hallucination-induced noise. Experiments on robot manipulation tasks involving contact-rich and precise tasks show that WorldSample improves policy success rate by 28% while reducing training steps by 59% compared with baselines. Furthermore, WorldSample improves world model visual fidelity by 19.4dB in PSNR and 0.47 in SSIM over demonstration-only post-training, validating the effectiveness of the real-synthetic loop for both policy and world model performance.

</details>


### [301] [Learning to Move Before Learning to Do: Task-Agnostic pretraining for VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02466)
*Junhao Shi,Siyin Wang,Xiaopeng Yu,Li Ji,Jingjing Gong,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出任务无关预训练（TAP）框架，通过解耦物理能力与语义对齐，利用廉价无标签交互数据学习可迁移的运动先验，仅需少量专家数据即可达到甚至超越大规模监督训练的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型受限于专家演示数据的稀缺性，其根本原因在于将物理能力（如何移动）与语义对齐（做什么）两个不同的学习目标混淆了。

Method: 提出分解假说，构建两阶段框架TAP：第一阶段通过自监督逆动力学目标从廉价无标签交互数据（含离轨轨迹和自主机器人探索）学习可迁移的运动先验；第二阶段用少量专家数据将运动先验与语言对齐。

Result: 在SIMPLER基准上，TAP匹配超100万条专家轨迹训练的模型性能，但数据量少数个数量级，比标准行为克隆绝对提升10%；在真实WidowX平台上，相机扰动下TAP保持25%成功率，而互联网规模基线降至0%。

Conclusion: 任务无关预训练能产生鲁棒且可迁移的物理表征，为具身AI的可扩展路径提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are fundamentally bottlenecked by the scarcity of expert demonstrations -- triplets of observations, instructions, and actions that are costly to collect at scale. We argue that this bottleneck stems from conflating two distinct learning objectives: acquiring physical competence (how to move) and acquiring semantic alignment (what to do). Crucially, only the latter requires language supervision. Building on this Decomposition Hypothesis, we propose Task-Agnostic Pretraining (TAP), a two-stage framework that first learns transferable motor priors from cheap, unlabeled interaction data -- including discarded off-task trajectories and autonomous robot play -- via a self-supervised Inverse Dynamics objective. A lightweight second stage then grounds these priors in language using minimal expert data. On the SIMPLER benchmark, TAP matches models trained on over 1M expert trajectories while using orders of magnitude less labeled data, yielding a 10% absolute gain over standard behavior cloning. On a real-world WidowX platform, TAP retains 25% success under camera perturbations where internet-scale baselines collapse to 0%, demonstrating that task-agnostic pretraining produces robust, transferable physical representations and offers a scalable path forward for Embodied AI.

</details>


### [302] [Learning Agile Intruder Interception using Differentiable Quadrotor Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02472)
*Michael Anoruo,Xiaoyu Tian,Abhishek Rathod,Timothy Naudet,Thomas Canchola,Eric Sturzinger,Kshitij Goel,Wennie Tabib*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用3D方向单位向量和拦截器状态学习控制策略的方法，通过可微四旋翼动力学和解析策略梯度实现最高10m/s的敏捷拦截，相比使用简化质点动力学的基线方法平均提升30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度强化学习方法假设可获取相对位置或距离信息，但在实际应用中（如使用无源单目相机传感器）这些信息难以直接获得，因此需要一种仅依赖方向信息即可实现拦截的控制策略。

Method: 利用可微四旋翼动力学模型，结合解析策略梯度方法，以入侵者的3D方向单位向量和拦截器状态作为输入，学习拦截控制策略。

Result: 所提方法在最高10m/s的拦截速度下，相比使用简化质点动力学的基线方法平均性能提升30%。

Conclusion: 仅利用方向信息即可实现高效的敏捷拦截，避免了传统方法对相对位置或距离信息的依赖，具有更强的实际应用可行性。

Abstract: This paper presents a methodology for learning a control policy to intercept an intruder using the 3D direction unit vector to the intruder and the interceptor state. Prior deep reinforcement learning approaches assume either relative position or distance to the intruder is available, but this information is not readily accessible in real-world applications that employ passive, monocular camera sensors. Instead, we propose a solution that leverages an analytical policy gradient method using differentiable quadrotor dynamics to learn agile interception at speeds up to 10 m/s. The proposed approach outperforms baseline methods that utilize simplified point mass dynamics by an average of 30%.

</details>


### [303] [QuadRocket: An Aerial Robotic Testbed for Adaptive Thrust-Vector Control of Rocket-Like Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02474)
*Pedro Santos,Joel Reis,Paulo Oliveira,Carlos Silvestre*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出QuadRocket——一种基于四旋翼的火箭原型，作为低成本、低风险的测试平台，用于验证运载火箭类系统的先进推力矢量控制策略。通过将四旋翼视为推力矢量执行器，设计了自适应反步控制器和动态面姿态控制器，实现了在未知常值扰动下的几乎全局轨迹跟踪。仿真与室内运动捕捉实验验证了其精确跟踪能力和扰动补偿效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运载火箭推力矢量控制策略的验证通常成本高昂、风险大，缺乏灵活且低成本的实验平台。

Method: 将QuadRocket建模为单轴对称刚体，采用二维球面上的降维姿态表示以利用轴向对称性并解耦偏航与推力矢量方向；设计自适应反步控制器实现未知常值扰动下的几乎全局轨迹跟踪，并通过控制点变换缓解非最小相位行为；将四旋翼作为推力矢量执行器，设计基于动态面的姿态控制器跟踪期望推力矢量，避免虚拟控制信号的显式微分。

Result: 仿真与室内运动捕捉实验结果表明，该平台实现了精确的轨迹跟踪和有效的扰动补偿，验证了QuadRocket作为推力矢量控制机器人车辆多功能测试平台的适用性。

Conclusion: QuadRocket成功为推力矢量控制策略提供了低成本、低风险的验证平台，实验结果证实了所提控制架构的有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents QuadRocket, a quadrotor-based rocket prototype that provides a low-cost, low-risk platform for validating advanced thrust-vector control strategies for launch vehicle-type systems. The prototype consists of a cylindrical main body mounted on top of a quadrotor through a universal joint, forming a flying inverted pendulum with non-negligible inertia. For control design, the coupled system is modeled as a single axisymmetric rigid body actuated by a vectored force applied along its longitudinal axis. A reduced-attitude representation on the two sphere is adopted to explicitly exploit the vehicle's axial symmetry and to decouple yaw from the thrust-vector direction. On this model, we derive an adaptive backstepping controller that achieves almost global trajectory tracking in the presence of unknown constant disturbances, while a control-point transformation mitigates non minimum-phase behavior. The quadrotor is then treated as a thrust vector actuator, and a dynamic-surface-based attitude controller is designed to track the desired thrust-vector, accounting for actuation dynamics and avoiding explicit differentiation of virtual control signals. The complete architecture is evaluated in simulation and validated experimentally in an indoor motion-capture arena. Results demonstrate accurate trajectory tracking, effective disturbance compensation, and confirm the suitability of the QuadRocket as a versatile testbed for thrust-vector-controlled robotic vehicles.

</details>


### [304] [Controllable Sim Agents with Behavior Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02496)
*Juanwu Lu,Junyu Zhu,Ziran Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CNeVA是一个可控的神经变分智能体框架，通过闭合形式的共轭变分更新从每通道折扣回报中推断高斯行为潜变量，结合整流流轨迹生成器与混合通道掩码课程训练，实现可解释的多维度可控交通仿真，在Waymo数据集上达到竞争性的逼真度并展示单调可控性，同时提出软资格门控解决奖励稀疏问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实交通仿真需要既能模仿记录行为又能沿可解释轴进行操控的智能体，以实现变量隔离、特定边缘案例复现和自动驾驶系统安全测试，而现有高排名模型缺乏每通道可控性。

Method: 1) 从每通道折扣回报中通过闭合形式共轭变分更新推断高斯行为潜变量；2) 采用整流流轨迹生成器，结合混合通道掩码课程训练实现无分类器引导；3) 提出软资格门控（平滑指数衰减替代硬二进制阈值）解决奖励信号稀疏问题。

Result: 在Waymo Open Motion Dataset上达到竞争性逼真度；速度和加速度操控产生单调响应且无失速诱导奖励破解；引入软资格门控后安全可控性单调且显著；实现了上下文残差回报度量下的可操控地图合规性。

Conclusion: CNeVA在保持逼真度的同时提供了现有模型缺乏的每通道可控性，且操控指标必须与物理合理性护栏一起解读以避免奖励破解混淆。

Abstract: Realistic traffic simulation requires agents that imitate logged behavior and can also be steered along interpretable axes. Such controllability enables engineers to isolate variables, reproduce specific edge cases, and test autonomous systems without real-world risk. We introduce Controllable Neural Variational Agents (CNeVA), a controllable simulated-agent framework that learns to infer a per-agent Gaussian behavior latent from per-channel discounted returns via a closed-form conjugate variational update, conditioning a rectified-flow trajectory generator trained on a mixed channel-mask curriculum for classifier-free guidance. To tackle scarcity in reward signals, we propose soft eligibility gates that replace hard binary thresholds with smooth exponential decay, preserving the gradient signal for near-threshold agents. On the Waymo Open Motion Dataset, CNeVA attains competitive realism on the benchmark while exposing per-channel controllability that the higher-ranked imitation models lack. Speed- and acceleration-based steering produces monotone responses without stall-induced reward hacking. Safety controllability is monotone and substantial with the introduction of soft eligibility. We manage to achieve steerable map compliance under a context-residual return measure. Furthermore, our experiment demonstrates that steering metrics must be read alongside physical-plausibility guardrails to avoid reward-hacking confounds.

</details>


### [305] [Embodied.cpp: A Portable Inference Runtime of Embodied AI Models on Heterogeneous Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02501)
*Ling Xu,Chuyu Han,Borui Li,Hao Wu,Shiqi Jiang,Ting Cao,Chuanyou Li,Sheng Zhong,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Embodied.cpp，一个面向具身AI模型的便携式C++推理运行时。它通过五层架构设计（输入适配器、序列构建器、骨干执行、头插件、部署适配器），支持多速率执行、延迟优先融合推理和可扩展算子/I/O，在异构边缘设备上实现了VLA模型和世界-动作模型的高效部署，在HY-VLA和pi0.5上分别达到100.0%和91.0%的任务成功率，并将WAM基准测试的块内存从312.2 MiB降至88.1 MiB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身AI模型（VLA和WAM）在实际部署中碎片化严重，依赖特定模型的Python栈、后端假设和机器人端胶水代码，尤其是在异构边缘设备上。现有的推理运行时主要为请求-响应服务设计，无法满足具身部署的运行时契约：闭环控制内的多速率执行、异构硬件上的延迟优先batch-1推理、超越固定token I/O的可扩展具身接口。

Method: 对代表性VLA模型和WAM进行架构分析，提取共享执行路径并组织为五层结构：输入适配器、序列构建器、骨干执行、头插件和部署适配器。提供模块化多速率执行、延迟优先融合推理、可扩展算子和I/O支持，通过统一后端抽象实现跨异构设备、机器人和模拟器的部署。

Result: 在HY-VLA和pi0.5两个VLA模型上实现闭环执行，任务成功率分别为100.0%和91.0%。在基于LingBot-VA Transformer块的WAM基准测试中，将块内存从312.2 MiB降至88.1 MiB。

Conclusion: Embodied.cpp在保持高精度的同时，显著提升了不同具身模型架构的部署效率。

Abstract: Embodied AI models now span vision-language-action (VLA) models and world-action models (WAMs), but practical deployment remains fragmented across model-specific Python stacks, backend assumptions, and robot-side glue code, especially on heterogeneous edge devices. Existing inference runtimes are designed mainly for request-response serving and therefore do not satisfy the runtime contract of embodied deployment: multi-rate execution inside closed-loop control, latency-first batch-1 inference on heterogeneous hardware, and extensible embodied interfaces beyond fixed token I/O. We present Embodied.cpp, a portable C++ inference runtime for embodied models. Based on an architectural analysis of representative VLA models and WAMs, Embodied.cpp captures a shared execution path and organizes it into five layers: input adapters, sequence builders, backbone execution, head plugins, and deployment adapters. The runtime provides modular multi-rate execution, latency-first fused inference, and extensible operator and I/O support, enabling deployment across heterogeneous devices, robots, and simulators through one backend abstraction. We evaluate Embodied.cpp on two VLA models, HY-VLA and pi0.5, and on a preliminary WAM benchmark using a LingBot-VA Transformer block. The VLA deployments achieve successful closed-loop execution with 100.0% and 91.0% task success rates, respectively. The WAM benchmark reduces block memory from 312.2 MiB to 88.1 MiB. These results show that Embodied.cpp improves deployment efficiency while preserving high accuracy across diverse embodied model architectures.

</details>


### [306] [VT-WAM: Visual-Tactile World Action Model for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02503)
*Shuai Tian,Yupeng Zheng,Yuhang Zheng,Songen Gu,Yujie Zang,Yuxing Qin,Weize Li,Haoran Li,Wenchao Ding,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VT-WAM提出了一种视觉-触觉世界动作模型，通过统一流匹配框架联合学习视觉预测、触觉变形预测和动作预测，在六项接触丰富操作任务中平均成功率达71.67%，显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-触觉策略通常直接将触觉观测输入动作预测，但很少在动作生成过程中建模触觉变形动态，而接触丰富的操作任务需要策略对局部变形、压力、滑移和摩擦等时间稀疏且视觉不可见的线索做出反应。

Method: (1) 非对称混合Transformer(MoT)注意力，桥接首帧视觉锚点与时间触觉动态；(2) 接触门控的动作-视觉-触觉注意力引导(AVTAG)，在接触阶段鼓励动作查询依赖触觉证据；统一流匹配框架联合学习三种预测。

Result: 六项真实世界接触丰富操作任务平均成功率71.67%，优于Fast-WAM（+26.67%）和OmniVTLA（+35.84%）。

Conclusion: 建模触觉变形动态和引导接触阶段触觉注意力对接触丰富操作任务均至关重要。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires policies to react to local deformation, pressure, slip, and friction, yet these cues are temporally sparse and often invisible in visual observations. Existing visual-tactile policies usually feed tactile observations directly into action prediction, but rarely model tactile deformation dynamics during action generation. In this paper, we introduce VT-WAM, a Visual-Tactile World Action Model that jointly learns future visual prediction, tactile deformation prediction, and action prediction within a unified flow matching framework. In particular, VT-WAM introduces (1) Asymmetric Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) attention to bridge a first-frame visual anchor with temporal tactile dynamics, and (2) contact-gated Action-Visual-Tactile Attention Guidance (AVTAG) to encourage action queries to rely on tactile evidence during contact phases. Across six real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, VT-WAM achieves a 71.67% average success rate, outperforming Fast-WAM by 26.67% and OmniVTLA by 35.84%. Ablations demonstrate that modeling tactile deformation dynamics and guiding contact-phase tactile attention are both important for contact-rich tasks. Project website: https://vt-wam.github.io/.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [307] [ExPerT: Personalizing LLM Responses to Users' Domain Expertise via Query-Wise Semantic and Keystroke Behavioral Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01242)
*Yeji Park,Jiwon Tark,Taesik Gong*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: ExPerT是一个查询级个性化框架，通过结合语义和键盘动态行为线索推断用户领域专业知识，并据此调整LLM响应的细节、术语和概念复杂度。用户实验表明，该框架在专业知识推断误差上降低65.7%，响应满意度提升17.52%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM个性化方法依赖静态用户画像或纯文本信号，无法捕捉用户在查询维度上的专业知识变化，导致响应与用户真实知识水平不匹配。

Method: 提出ExPerT框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 语义-行为专业知识推断模块，通过上下文学习提示LLM联合分析查询文本和键盘动力学特征；(2) 专业知识条件化响应生成，根据推断的专业水平动态调整响应的详细程度、术语使用和概念复杂度。

Result: 在40名参与者、1270条查询的用户研究中，ExPerT将专业知识推断误差降低65.7%（MAE=0.398 vs. 1.162），响应满意度在5点Likert量表上提升17.52%（从3.71到4.36）。

Conclusion: 语义线索与行为线索（如键盘动力学）的融合能有效推断用户查询级专业知识，从而实现更精准的LLM响应个性化。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used by end users, yet existing personalization methods relying on static profiles or text-only signals fail to capture query-specific expertise variation. We present ExPerT, a query-wise personalization framework that adapts LLM responses to users' query domain expertise by combining semantic and behavioral cues. ExPerT consists of two key components: (i) a semantic-behavioral expertise inference module that jointly interprets query text and keystroke dynamics via in-context LLM prompting, and (ii) an expertise-conditioned response generation that adapts the level of detail, terminology, and conceptual complexity. Our user study with 40 participants and 1270 queries demonstrated that ExPerT reduced expertise inference error by 65.7% compared to the strongest baseline (MAE = 0.398 vs. 1.162) and improved response satisfaction by 17.52% (from 3.71 to 4.36) on a 5-point Likert scale.

</details>


### [308] [Mitigating Confirmation Bias through Hand-Drawing Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01359)
*Chenyu Lin,Cindy Xiong,Icy Zhang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过手绘视频展示数据可视化构建过程，能帮助观者更准确地解读数据，减少确认偏误的影响，即使数据与观者既有信念相冲突时也有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据可视化解读常受观者先验信念影响而产生确认偏误，导致错误决策。现有研究缺乏有效缓解该偏误的方法，因此需要探索新的可视化呈现方式。

Method: 开展对比实验，将静态柱状图与手绘构建过程的视频进行对照，在数据分别吻合和挑战参与者先验信念两种情境下，测量解读准确率和偏误程度。

Result: 手绘视频组在数据与先验信念冲突时仍能准确解读，减少了信念一致性错误，增加了克服信念的正确响应。

Conclusion: 暴露可视化构建过程有助于提升数据推理准确性、缓解确认偏误，为设计偏误缓解型数据界面提供了新思路。

Abstract: Understanding data visualizations is essential for informed decision-making, yet interpretation is often shaped and even distorted by prior beliefs. We investigate whether an embodied pedagogical approach, in which viewers observe the dynamic hand-drawing of a visualization, can mitigate confirmation bias and improve interpretation accuracy. We conducted a study comparing static bar charts to videos in which charts are constructed through hand-drawing, across contexts that either align with or challenge participants' prior beliefs. The results indicate that hand-drawn videos helped participants accurately interpret data, even when the data conflicted with their prior beliefs. This approach also reduced belief-consistent errors and increased belief-overriding responses. These findings suggest that exposing the construction process of a visualization supports more accurate reasoning and mitigates the influence of confirmation bias. Consequently, this work introduces a promising design space for bias-mitigating data interfaces.

</details>


### [309] [Mind the Trust Gap: Identifying (Mis)alignments in Teacher-Student Views Toward Control and Agency in K-12 Classroom AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01506)
*Tomohiro Nagashima,Lisa Siegrist,Niklas Scholz,Shintaro Sato,Martina Vincoli,Man Su*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过故事板速配实验，发现德国K-12学生与教师在AI决策控制权、信任度及社会情感学习方面存在显著观点错位，揭示了师生关系对AI互动偏好的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI技术已融入课堂环境，但之前研究未能充分揭示教师与学生（尤其是K-12阶段）在AI使用上的观点是否对齐，亟需深入理解不同利益相关者的视角差异。

Method: 在德国招募16名K-12学生和15名教师，采用故事板（storyboard）驱动的速配研究（speed-dating study），通过显式配对匹配分析（explicit pair-matching analysis）比较双方在AI决策控制权上的观点。

Result: 学生与教师在AI信任程度、社会情感学习方面存在显著观点错位；发现师生关系（非AI使用场景下）对AI互动偏好和观点形成具有重要影响。

Conclusion: 师生-AI互动的偏好具有复杂性，取决于利益相关者间的动态关系；需弥合观点差距，制定针对性策略。

Abstract: As Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based technologies have been integrated into school classrooms where multiple stakeholders (with different roles) interact with each other, it is critical to deeply understand stakeholder views in the classroom. In particular, prior work has not fully uncovered how teachers' and school students' views might or might not align well with each other, especially in K-12 classrooms. We conducted a speed-dating study using storyboards with 16 school students and 15 school teachers in Germany to investigate alignments and misalignments between their views on student-AI decision-making control in K-12 classroom. Through an explicit pair-matching analysis, we found that students and teachers had misaligned views on several key topics, including how much they trust AI and social and emotional aspects of student learning with AI. Findings also revealed the importance of teacher-student relationships outside of AI use that shape stakeholders' views and interactions. We discuss potential reasons for the observed misaligned views and strategies to fill the perspective gaps. This study illustrates the complexities of preferences in teacher-student-AI interactions that depend on the dynamic relations among the stakeholders.

</details>


### [310] [OrchestrXR: A Multi-Agent System for Idea-to-Prototype XR Study Authoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01588)
*Shuqi Liao,Chenfei Zhu,Karthik Ramani,Voicu Popescu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: OrchestrXR 是一个多智体人-AI协作工作流，用于将早期XR研究想法快速转化为可运行的Unity原型，支持研究设计、场景生成和交互生成的渐进式可控流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前从XR研究想法到可运行原型的流程在实验设计、场景构建和交互实现三个环节上高度碎片化，缺乏统一的工具支持，研究者需要跨多个工具和阶段手动转换。

Method: 提出多智体人-AI协作工作流OrchestrXR，通过结构化Schema、多智体编排和交互式人-Agent界面，将XR研究创作分解为研究设计、场景生成和交互生成三个阶段，最终输出Unity原型。

Result: 12名XR研究者的用户研究表明，OrchestrXR能为早期XR研究创作提供有效支持，并在各阶段之间保持强烈的意图一致性。

Conclusion: OrchestrXR验证了多智体人-AI协作范式在XR研究原型快速迭代中的可行性，为自动化XR研究创作提供了新的可控工作流方向。

Abstract: Extended Reality (XR) has become an important interaction paradigm in Human-Computer Interaction (HCI). XR studies are used to investigate interaction, perception, and user behavior in immersive environments, and typically involve experimental tasks, 3D scenes, and interactive logic. However, turning an initial XR study idea into a runnable prototype remains fragmented across study design, scene construction, and interaction implementation. We present OrchestrXR, a multi-agent human-AI workflow for early-stage idea-to-prototype XR study authoring. Rather than treating XR study creation as one-shot generation, OrchestrXR supports a controllable workflow across study design, scene generation, and interaction generation through structured schemas, multi-agent orchestration, and interactive human-agent interfaces, producing a Unity-based prototype from a researcher's idea. A user study with 12 XR researchers suggests that OrchestrXR provides effective support for early-stage XR study authoring with strong intent preservation across stages.

</details>


### [311] [Made to Feel: How Designers Bring Emotions into Affective Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01593)
*Yixin Bai,Ziyi Wang,Keke Wu,Fumeng Yang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对15位可视化从业者的半结构化访谈，本文揭示了情感在设计过程中的三种功能（入口、参与、结果）、设计师处理情感的三个层面（数据、设计、受众）及其设计策略，以及伦理考量。研究发现情感意图常在设计过程中涌现而非预先规划，情感影响源于设计选择的累积而非孤立视觉元素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感化可视化在研究中日益受到关注，但设计师如何在可视化工作中融入情感这一过程尚未被探索。

Method: 对15位可视化从业者进行半结构化访谈，采用混合主题分析方法进行分析。

Result: 识别出情感的三种功能（入口、参与、结果）、设计师处理情感的三个层面（数据、设计、受众）及相应设计策略、伦理考量；发现情感意图多在过程中涌现，情感影响源于设计选择的累积效应。

Conclusion: 情感意图往往在设计过程中自然涌现而非预先计划，情感影响是累积性设计选择的结果；评估是参与者指出的关键挑战。

Abstract: Affective visualization is increasingly studied in visualization research, yet how designers bring emotions into their visualization work remains unexplored. This paper addresses this gap through semi-structured interviews with 15 visualization practitioners. Using hybrid thematic analysis, we identify: (1) three functions that emotions can serve for viewers (entry, engagement, outcome); (2) three facets of how designers work with emotion (data, design, audience), along with design strategies; and (3) ethical considerations in the design process. We also observe that affective intent often emerges during the design process rather than being planned from the outset, and that emotional impact arises from accumulated design choices rather than isolated visual elements. Finally, we highlight evaluation as a key challenge identified by our participants.

</details>


### [312] [Evaluating Glanceable Multi-Device Family Health Tracking with Smartwatches and Home Displays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01618)
*Lucas M. Silva,Evropi Stefanidi,Aehong Min,Franceli L. Cibrian,Jesus A. Beltran,Cassie Zeiler,Sabrina E. B. Schuck,Kimberley D. Lakes,Gillian R. Hayes,Daniel A. Epstein*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究比较了智能手表、家庭显示屏以及两者组合三种设计对家庭健康追踪的影响，发现多设备组合能提升情绪和目标的追踪频率，支持家庭协作和不同成员的使用偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泛在计算领域已有许多针对个人健康追踪的设备研究，但对多设备设计如何支持家庭整体健康管理（家庭信息学）的了解较少，而家庭健康管理本质上是协作性的。

Method: 12个家庭共44人参与了为期9周的交替使用实验，对比智能手表单独、家庭显示屏单独以及两者组合三种设计方案，通过日志分析和访谈进行数据收集。

Result: 有家庭显示屏时情绪和目标的追踪频率显著高于仅用智能手表；追踪活动集中在下午、周末下降、家中2.6倍更频繁；儿童比成人更持续追踪。智能手表支持外出时的即兴追踪和感知，家庭显示屏则提醒家人在日常活动中自我追踪和协作，并包容不使用可穿戴设备的成员。

Conclusion: 多设备冗余设计能适应同一家庭成员在日常习惯、移动模式和设备偏好上的多样性，未来应通过包容性、可一瞥即得且适应性强的泛在数据共享来支持家庭信息学。

Abstract: While ubiquitous computing research has explored diverse devices for personal health tracking, we know less about multi-device designs for family informatics, where health management is inherently collaborative. To understand how families adopt and perceive ubiquitous access to shared health data across contexts, we evaluated smartwatch-only, home display-only, and combined designs for tracking moods and goals, domains central to family health behavior regulation. 44 people across 12 families alternated between these designs over nine weeks. Log analysis revealed that mood tracking and goal reporting were significantly more frequent with the home display present compared to smartwatch-only use, despite an overall decline in mood tracking over time. Tracking peaked in afternoons, dropped on weekends, and occurred 2.6X more at home, with children tracking more consistently than adults across all designs. From interview analysis, we learned how family data glanceability on smartwatches supported opportunistic tracking and awareness while apart, whereas displays reminded families to self-track and collaborate during home routines including members that avoided wearables (e.g., non-participants). Multi-device redundancy accommodated diversity in routines, mobility patterns, and device preferences among members in the same family. We discuss opportunities for multi-device family informatics that accommodates different preferences through inclusive, glanceable, and adaptable ubiquitous data sharing.

</details>


### [313] [From Answer Generators to Reasoning Facilitators: Designing AI Tutors for Mathematical Reasoning in High-Stakes Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01692)
*Yuming Feng,Yuan Tian,Erica Zhao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍AITutor系统，通过将理论教学机制转化为UI功能，在中考备考学生中进行部署研究。学生面对时间压力会抵制苏格拉底式对话，转而使用"答案优先"捷径作为诊断检查点。研究提出"以推理为中心的产品循环"，为在真实场景中支持数学推理检查、局部修复、课程验证和延迟检索的AI设计提供启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在教育技术中的快速整合可能将数学学习简化为单纯的答案生成，需要探索如何设计AI系统真正支持学生的数学推理过程而非取代它。

Method: 采用混合方法三角验证：通过AITutor系统（将理论教学机制转化为具体UI功能）进行生成研究、可用性研究，以及在12名初三学生中开展实地部署，收集7,379条遥测事件、8次情境观察和10次访谈数据。

Result: 学生在高时间压力下主动抵制传统苏格拉底式对话，将"答案优先"捷径重新用作重要的诊断检查点；分层式工作示例、步骤关联视觉锚定和元认知支架等功能降低了推理修复的交互成本。

Conclusion: 提出"以推理为中心的产品循环"，为设计能在真实场景中结构化支持数学推理检查、局部修复、课程验证和延迟检索的AI系统提供可操作的设计启示。

Abstract: The rapid integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into educational technology threatens to reduce mathematical learning to mere answer generation. This paper presents a generative study, usability study, and 12-participant field deployment of AITutor, an interactive system that translates theoretical pedagogical mechanisms into concrete user interface features. We explore how junior-high students preparing for high-stakes exams (Zhongkao) interact with AI tutoring. Through mixed-methods triangulation (7,379 telemetry events, 8 contextual observations, 10 interviews), we reveal that students actively resist traditional Socratic dialogue under time pressure, repurposing "answer-first" shortcuts as vital diagnostic checkpoints. We demonstrate how features like layered worked examples, step-linked visual grounding, and metacognitive scaffolding lower the interaction cost of reasoning repair. We contribute a "Reasoning-Centered Product Loop," offering actionable implications for designing AI that structurally supports the inspection, local repair, curriculum verification, and delayed retrieval of mathematical reasoning in the wild.

</details>


### [314] [Adapting CCDF Plots for Visualizing Ordinal Regression Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01747)
*Abhraneel Sarma*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文指出累积链接序数回归模型在分析序数数据（如李克特量表）时具有优势，但因结果可视化困难而未被广泛采用。作者提出使用修正互补累积分布函数（mCCDF）图来直观展示序数回归模型的结果，并能像传统将序数数据视为度量数据的分析方法一样有效传达关键结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序数回归模型在统计上比线性模型或ANOVA更适合分析序数数据（如李克特量表），但由于结果难以可视化和直观传达，该方法的采用率有限。作者希望解决这一可视化障碍，促进更合适的统计方法在可视化及相关领域的应用。

Method: 提出使用修正互补累积分布函数（mCCDF）图来可视化序数回归模型的结果，通过这种方式直观呈现模型的关键发现。

Result: mCCDF图能够以与将序数数据视为度量数据的传统分析方法同等有效的方式，传达序数回归模型的关键结论。

Conclusion: mCCDF图是一种有效的可视化工具，可以帮助研究者更直观地呈现和沟通序数回归模型的分析结果，有望推动该方法在可视化及相关领域的更广泛采用。

Abstract: Cumulative-link ordinal regression models are an alternative approach for analysing ordinal data such as Likert items, which are widely used in Visualization (and other related fields like HCI, psychology etc.). There are many researchers who are strong proponents of this approach, as it makes less stringent assumptions about the data, compared to the more commonly used linear model or ANOVA. Yet, ordinal regression models have seen limited adoption. I posit that one possible reason for this might be due to the difficulty in visually representing the results from such models, and in communicating the key takeaways in an intuitive manner. I propose the use of (modified) Complementary Cumulative Distribution Function (mCCDF) plots to visualize the results of ordinal regression models, and demonstrate how the same takeaways that researchers present from analyses which treat ordinal data as metric can be easily communicated using mCCDFs.

</details>


### [315] [A Social Norms Approach to Youth Social Media Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01807)
*JaeWon Kim*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文指出，年轻人难以在社交媒体上实现真实自我表达、减少评判和建立信任，根本原因不是个人选择问题，而是平台规范、同伴感知和集体信念等社会规范层面的障碍。论文提出三个核心观点：平台规范导致集体无知（青年私下反对却公开遵从）；设计干预受现有规范制约，隐私等关系目标应作为社会规范而非个人设置；对“社交媒体”的狭隘认知限制了政策和设计创新。研究通过让青年直接参与设计和构建独立平台来验证这些观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 年轻人渴望在社交媒体上实现真实表达、减少评判和建立信任，但现实中却难以实现。论文认为这一困境的根源不是个人能力或意愿问题，而是社会规范层面的系统性障碍，包括平台规范对个人行为的约束、设计干预受现有规范制约，以及社会对“社交媒体”的狭隘定义限制了创新空间。

Method: 采用社会规范研究方法论，围绕三个核心主张展开理论论证，并让青年直接参与设计和构建一个基于证据的独立社交平台，该平台通过功能设计持续传递“建立可信联系是空间的核心目的”这一信号。

Result: 揭示了平台规范导致青年陷入集体无知的困境（公开遵从私下反对的行为模式）；证明了设计干预的有效性取决于其所在的社会规范环境；指出对社交媒体狭隘的社会定义限制了政策和设计创新的可能性。

Conclusion: 要解决青年在社交媒体上的信任与真实表达问题，需要从社会规范层面进行干预，而非仅依赖个人选择或技术修复。通过让青年参与设计和构建全新的独立平台，可以从根本上改变社交媒体的规范体系，实现真正的可信连接。

Abstract: Young people consistently say they want authentic self-expression, less judgment, and more interpersonal trust on social media, yet they rarely manage to engage that way. My dissertation argues that the obstacle is normative rather than individual: how youth engage is governed less by personal choice than by platform norms, peer perception, and beliefs about how others behave. I take a social norms approach to youth social media design organized around three claims. First, platform norms constrain individual behavior, producing a pluralistic ignorance in which youth enact norms they privately reject. Second, design interventions are themselves shaped by existing norms, so whether a feature works depends on the environment around it, which means relational goals such as privacy must be treated as social norms rather than individual settings. Third, a societal norm about what ``social media'' is -- equating it with a few mainstream platforms -- confines policy and design to mitigating those platforms rather than actively envisioning supportive alternatives. Together these claims motivate my dissertation research: engaging youth directly in designing and building an evidence-based independent platform whose features consistently signal that building trusted connections is what the space is for.

</details>


### [316] [Visual Analytics of Neighborhood Attribute Profiles for Exploring Structural Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02163)
*Kohei Arimoto,Masahiko Itoh*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于降维的可视分析方法，揭示属性网络中节点邻域属性特征空间的实际拓扑结构。通过分析企业间交易网络发现，结构角色形成具有密度偏差的非线性流形，且行业分类与结构角色之间存在不一致性，表明假设相同语义隐含相似结构角色可能存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示学习方法隐式假设特征空间均匀且连续，但真实网络中节点邻域属性特征空间的实际拓扑结构尚不清晰，需要对高维特征空间的真实结构进行探索。

Method: 采用降维（dimensionality reduction）的可视分析方法，对节点邻域属性特征（neighborhood attribute profiles）构成的高维特征空间进行可视化分析。

Result: （1）供应链层级呈现连续过渡；（2）通用语义下视为相同的类别在实际交易网络中可被清晰区分；（3）单一行业标签可能分裂成多个区域。

Conclusion: 假设相同语义隐含相似结构角色可能存在局限，需要发展与流形拓扑对齐的新相似性度量。

Abstract: Exploring similar nodes in attributed networks represents a key challenge in data mining. While recent representation learning methods embed networks into low-dimensional vectors, they often implicitly assume a uniform and continuous feature space. This paper proposes a visual analytics approach using dimensionality reduction to help clarify the true topological structure of high-dimensional feature spaces formed by nodes' neighborhood attribute profiles. Analyzing inter-firm transaction networks indicates that structural roles can form complex, non-linear manifolds with density biases. Comparing this feature space with industry classifications suggested: (1) supply chain hierarchies transition continuously; (2) categories treated identically under general semantics can be clearly separated by actual transaction networks; and (3) a single industry label may fragment into multiple regions. These findings suggest potential limitations in assuming identical semantics imply similar structural roles and highlight the possible need for new similarity metrics aligned with manifold topology.

</details>


### [317] [Synthetic Contact with AI Reduces Cross-Partisan Animosity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02181)
*Benjamin Lira,Noah Castelo,Stefano Puntoni,Olivier Toubia*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过五项预注册研究（总N=3,960）发现，与代表政治外群体的AI聊天机器人进行简短对话（合成接触）能够替代人们回避的真实跨党派接触：这种接触降低了进入门槛（人们宁愿忍受近两倍的死亡沉思来避免与人接触）、纠正了对立党派的态度误解（一次10分钟对话即可纠正民主党对共和党环保态度的严重误判并改善情感）、促进行为改变（参与者在移民议题上选择与真实对立党派对话的可能性提高6个百分点），但效果在一周内大部分消退，仅在最极端党派成员中保留残余效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去三十年间美国两党之间的情感温度急剧下降，但有意义的跨党派接触仍然稀缺，部分原因是人们主动回避这种接触。研究者试图探索AI聊天机器人是否能替代人们所回避的真实人际接触，作为一种可扩展的干预手段。

Method: 五项预注册实验（总N=3,960名美国党派人士）。实验比较了人们对AI外群体与人类外群体伙伴的回避意愿；采用被试内设计检验单次10分钟对话对态度认知的纠正效果；采用三臂实验排除纯参与和社交性作为驱动因素；测量行为层面的改变（是否愿意与真实对立党派对话）；追踪一周后的效果持续性，并分析对话内容以区分信息与友好性的作用。

Result: 人们宁愿忍受近两倍的死亡沉思来避免与人类外群体互动而非AI；基线时民主党将共和党环保消费态度错误地估计超过一个标准差（足以将共和党从支持翻转为反对），一次10分钟AI对话即可纠正；对话使参与者选择与真实对立党派对话的可能性提高6个百分点；温暖效果在即时复现中成立，但一周内大部分消退，仅最极端党派成员有残余效应；对话内容分析表明信息传递比友好性更能区分外群体AI与对照AI。

Conclusion: 合成接触（与AI外群体聊天机器人对话）是一种可扩展、具有行为影响力且被广泛接受的跨党派接触形式，尽管其长期效果有限，但在降低接触门槛和纠正错误认知方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: Americans' warmth toward members of the opposing political party has fallen sharply over the past three decades -- yet meaningful cross-partisan contact remains scarce, in part because people actively avoid it. Across five preregistered studies (total N = 3,960 U.S. partisans), we test whether brief conversations with AI chatbots representing the political outgroup can substitute for the contact people shun. Synthetic contact first lowers the barrier to entry: partisans would endure almost twice as long contemplating their own mortality to avoid a human outgroup partner as an AI one. These conversations then correct the misperceptions that fuel division. At baseline, Democrats placed Republicans more than a standard deviation past their actual position on environmental consumption attitudes -- enough to flip the average Republican from supportive to opposed -- and a single ten-minute conversation with an outgroup chatbot corrected those beliefs and warmed affect in a within-person study of both parties. A three-arm experiment ruled out pure engagement and sociality as drivers. Synthetic contact also moved behavior, in a sample of both parties and on a more affectively charged issue: participants who spoke with an outgroup bot about immigration were six percentage points more likely than controls to choose to have a real conversation with a partisan from the other side. A final study tested whether these gains last: the warmth effect replicated immediately in a new sample; most of it faded within a week, with a small residual concentrated among the most extreme partisans. Analyzing conversation content showed that information, more than friendliness, distinguishes outgroup bots from control chatbots. Together, these findings establish synthetic contact as a scalable, behaviorally consequential, and -- unlike face-to-face contact -- widely acceptable form of cross-partisan engagement.

</details>


### [318] [What Types of Human-AI Teams Exist?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02198)
*Nathan Hughes,Ibrahim Habli*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文分析了53篇人-AI团队文献，基于心理学团队分类法将其分为5个聚类（AI助手、临时依赖、临时强制依赖、配对平等、群体平等），揭示了不同团队类型的差异，并提供了识别、报告和综合该领域的指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人-AI团队研究在多个领域广泛开展，但缺乏统一的分类框架来理解不同类型的团队及其相似性/差异性，导致跨论文的研究见解是否可迁移存疑。

Method: 对53篇人-AI团队论文进行分析，基于心理学团队分类法将其归类为5个聚类（AI Assistant, Ad-hoc Dependency, Ad-hoc Forced Dependency, Paired Equanimity, Group Equanimity）。

Result: 识别出5个不同的团队类型聚类，每个聚类代表独特的整体团队层级特征，表明在同一个定义下存在多种不同的团队类型。

Conclusion: 提供了识别研究中所用人-AI团队类型的指导、研究报告检查清单，以及进一步综合该领域的方向。

Abstract: Human-AI teaming has received increasing attention in the literature. However, the range of studies conducted in multiple domains make it difficult to understand what types of teams are being studied, and in what ways are they similar/different from one another. In this study, we analyse 53 papers on human-AI teams and categorise them into five main clusters based on psychological taxonomies of teaming; AI Assistant, Ad-hoc Dependency, Ad-hoc Forced Dependency, Paired Equanimity, and Group Equanimity. Each cluster represents a unique combination of holistic team-level characteristics, indicating there are multiple disparate team types studied under the same definition. In turn, this raises the question of whether insights are truly transferable between papers. We conclude with guidance on how to identify the types of human-AI teams studied, a checklist for reporting a human-AI team in research work, and ways in which the field can be further synthesised.

</details>


### [319] [Personality Without Persons? A Psychometric Critique of Big Five Testing in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02325)
*Kim Zierahn,Cristina Cachero,Anna Korhonen,Nuria Oliver*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文对大型语言模型（LLMs）中的大五人格测量进行了系统的心理测量学评估，发现：1）为LLM改编的大五项目能达到足够的内容效度，但原始人类项目不行；2）大五量表无法捕捉LLM间的有意义差异（仅解释3%的方差）；3）LLM的回答未能恢复大五的五因子结构，其中四个因子高度相关（r>=0.92）；4）指令微调使LLM向社会期望特质偏移。结论认为大五人格分数在LLM中并未测量到与人类人格等同的构念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类人格量表被越来越多地用于表征LLM、比较系统和指导治理决策，但这些量表是为人类开发和验证的，是否适用于LLM尚不清楚。作者旨在通过系统的心理测量学评估来检验这一适用性。

Method: 作者对五个候选大五人格量表进行内容效度评估，选出最优量表后对来自49个模型家族的244个不同LLM模型进行施测。通过方差分析评估模型间差异，通过因子分析检验五因子结构是否恢复，并比较基础模型与指令微调模型的得分差异。

Result: 1）为LLM改编的大五项目内容效度充足，但原始人类项目不足；2）模型间方差仅占总得分方差的3%，LLM间差异极小；3）大五五因子结构未恢复，四个因子高度相关（r>=0.92）并合并为一个因子；4）指令微调使LLM向社会期望特质系统性偏移。

Conclusion: 大五人格分数在LLM中并未测量到与人类人格等同的构念，直接将人类人格框架应用于LLM会产生误导性表征。需要开发专门针对LLM的评估框架，而非未经验证地采用人类构念。

Abstract: Human personality inventories are increasingly used to characterize large language models (LLMs), compare systems, and inform downstream governance claims. Yet, these inventories were developed and validated for humans, and it remains unclear whether they apply to LLMs. We present a systematic psychometric evaluation of Big Five personality measurements in LLMs. We ask three research questions: Do Big Five inventories a) appropriately describe LLMs, b) capture inter-individual differences across models, and c) reflect internal factors consistent with human personality. We assess content validity of five candidate Big Five inventories and administer the winning inventory to N = 244 different models spanning 49 model families. First, we found that Big Five items adapted for LLMs can reach sufficient content validity, while original human-developed items did not. Second, Big Five inventories did not capture meaningful differences between LLMs: We found low variability between models, accounting for only 3% of total score variance. Third, LLMs responses did not recover the Big Five five-factor structure with four of the Big Five facets collapsing into one (r >= .92). Direct comparisons between base and instruction-tuned model variants suggested that alignment training systematically shifted Big Five scores toward socially desirable traits. These findings demonstrate that Big Five scores do not measure a construct equivalent to human personality in LLMs. Applying human personality frameworks to LLMs produces misleading characterizations used to benchmark, compare, and govern LLMs. We highlight the need for evaluation frameworks that are developed for LLMs, rather than adopting human constructs without validation.

</details>


### [320] [Data Comics for Education: Evaluating Effectiveness, Benefits, and the Ethics of AI-Assisted Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02361)
*Zirui Shan,Vanessa Echeverria,Yuheng Li,Yi-Shan Tsai,Roberto Martinez-Maldonado*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过60名大学生的实验，研究发现使用生成式AI辅助创作的数据漫画相比传统可视化图表，能显著提升学生的信息检索和理解能力，尤其在洞察理解任务中表现突出，且不受学生先验可视化素养影响。数据漫画被认为更具吸引力和易理解性，但存在AI生成错误信息和所有权问题的担忧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生在解读数据可视化方面存在可视化素养不足的问题，数据漫画作为增强理解和参与度的有前景媒介，但其教育价值缺乏实证检验，且创作成本高。生成式AI的发展为解决这一挑战提供了可扩展的方案。

Method: 采用被试内设计，对60名大学生进行实验，比较传统可视化图表与借助生成式AI工具创建的数据漫画在信息检索和理解任务上的表现。测量指标包括任务表现、用户主观评价及先验可视化素养水平。

Result: 学生在数据漫画条件下表现持续优于传统可视化图表，尤其在洞察理解任务中效果显著，且该优势独立于学生的先验可视化素养。学生认为数据漫画更具吸引力和易理解性，但同时对AI生成错误信息和所有权表示担忧。

Conclusion: 数据漫画在教育领域作为数据沟通工具具有潜在有效性，但需要解决与AI辅助创作相关的伦理问题（如错误信息和所有权）。

Abstract: In today's data-driven world, students often struggle with interpreting visualisations due to limited visualisation literacy. Data comics have emerged as a promising medium to enhance engagement and understanding, but their educational value has seen little empirical examination, partly due to the effort required to create them. Recent advances in Generative AI (GenAI) offer a scalable solution to this challenge. We conducted a within-subjects study with 60 university students, comparing conventional visualisations with data comics, created with assistance from GenAI tools, across information retrieval and comprehension tasks. Students consistently performed better with data comics, particularly in insight comprehension tasks, independent of prior visualisation literacy. Students also commented data comics as more engaging and easier to understand, though concerns were raised about GenAI-driven misinformation and ownership. Our findings highlight the potential of data comics as a potentially effective tool for data communication in education, while underscoring the need to address ethical concerns related to AI-assisted creation.

</details>


### [321] [Physical surfaces make touch interactions in virtual reality precise, efficient, and bimanual](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02430)
*Wen Ying,Seongkook Heo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了VR系统中不同手部接地触觉反馈类型（无触觉反馈、触觉反馈、物理表面）对高精度触摸任务（选择、追踪、素描）性能的影响，发现便携式物理表面在精度、效率和绘图质量上表现最佳，并提升了双手利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VR系统中的空中手势缺乏触觉反馈和物理参考表面，导致精确高效的触摸交互困难，因此需要研究不同手部接地触觉反馈对高精度任务性能的影响。

Method: 设计了三种触觉反馈条件（无触觉反馈、触觉反馈、物理表面），通过选择、追踪、素描等任务对比评估触摸性能。

Result: 便携式物理表面实现了最佳的选择精度、追踪效率和素描质量，并增加了双手利用率，用户更偏好物理表面交互，认为其提供更好的信心和控制感。

Conclusion: 物理表面触觉反馈显著提升了高精度VR触摸任务的性能，建议在需要精细操作的VR交互中引入便携式物理表面。

Abstract: Virtual reality (VR) systems can enable convenient hand-based interactions across diverse work scenarios. However, mid-air gestures lack tactile feedback and a physical reference surface to support the hand. This absence of haptic grounding can cause significant challenges in achieving precise and efficient touch interactions. This paper investigates the effect of different types of hand-grounded haptic feedback on the touch performance of VR tasks that demand high precision, such as selecting, tracing, and sketching. We compared three levels of haptic feedback: 1) No Haptic Feedback, where only visual feedback was provided; 2) Tactile Feedback, where users received vibrotactile and pressure feedback upon touching a virtual surface; 3) Physical Surface, where users interacted with a portable and tangible surface.
  Our study found that portable physical surfaces enabled the best selection precision, tracing efficiency, and sketch quality. Furthermore, participants showed increased bimanual hand utilization when engaging with a physical surface during tasks. These observed behaviors corresponded to participants' preference for interacting with physical surfaces, attributed to a better sense of confidence and control.

</details>


### [322] [When Do LLM Personas Support Visualization Design? A Cross-Model Study of Color Assignment and Chart Choice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02455)
*Shahreen Salim,Klaus Mueller*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了大语言模型人格角色在可视化设计中的稳定性，发现在色彩分配任务中人格-色彩关联高度依赖模型选择，在图表偏好任务中任务上下文比人格更能预测顶级选择，因此LLM角色应作为探索性工具而非人类参与者的替代品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM人格角色被越来越多地用于近似多样化用户进行可视化设计评估，但尚不清楚这些输出反映的是稳定的人格效应还是模型选择与任务框架的伪影，本研究旨在厘清这一问题。

Method: 使用43个Big Five人格剖面，在GPT-4o-mini、GPT-4.1-mini和GPT-5-mini三个模型上开展两项任务：1）抽象与具体概念的色彩分配；2）不同任务上下文下的图表惯用语偏好评分。通过人格-色彩耦合的一致性、概念类型的影响以及无人格基线对比进行分析。

Result: 人格-色彩关联高度依赖模型配置：GPT-4o-mini中完全不相关，GPT-4.1-mini中全部一致，GPT-5-mini中仅2/6概念相关；抽象概念中人格比模型身份更能解释色调变异；图表选择中无人格基线在9个模型-上下文组合的8个中恢复了相同的顶级选择，表明任务上下文比人格更能驱动排名第一的选择。

Conclusion: LLM人格角色应作为可视化设计的探索性探针，而非人类参与者的替代品；未来研究需要进行多模型测试、概念类型分拆以及无人格基线对比。

Abstract: Large language model personas are increasingly used to approximate diverse users during early-stage visualization design, but it remains unclear whether persona-conditioned outputs reflect stable personality effects or artifacts of model choice and task framing. We examine this question across two visualization-relevant tasks: color assignment for abstract and concrete concepts, and chart-idiom preference ratings across task contexts. Using 43 Big Five profiles across GPT-4o-mini, GPT-4.1-mini, and GPT-5-mini, we find that personality-color coupling is highly model-configuration dependent: absent in GPT-4o-mini for all six concepts, consistent in GPT-4.1-mini across all six, and partial in GPT-5-mini for two of six. Concept type further shapes the signal: for abstract concepts, personality explains more hue variance than model identity, while concrete concepts show smaller and comparable effects. In chart choice, trait-aligned cluster aggregation produces stable top-idiom rankings across all nine cluster-context combinations, but a no-persona baseline recovers the same top choice in 8 of 9 model-context cells, indicating that task context drives rank-1 selection more than personality. These findings position LLM personas as exploratory probes for visualization design, not substitutes for human participants, and motivate multi-model testing, concept-type disaggregation, and no-persona baselines in future studies.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [323] [Multilayer Q-Matrix-Embedded Neural Network for Cognitive Diagnosis (M-QCDNet): Structure-Aware Deep Learning Architecture for Psychometric Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01278)
*Yiyao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了多层Q矩阵嵌入神经网络（M-QCDNet），通过将Q矩阵作为结构先验嵌入深度神经网络，结合认知诊断模型的可解释性与深度学习灵活性，并设计了带L2惩罚的损失函数和可解释对齐评估指标，实现兼具预测性能和结构一致性的认知诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知诊断模型（CDMs）具有良好的结构可解释性但缺乏深度学习的灵活性与表达能力，而深度神经网络虽然预测性能强但缺乏心理测量学所需的透明性和可解释性。如何将两者优势结合，在保持认知诊断理论一致性的同时提升模型性能，是核心研究动机。

Method: 1) 将Q矩阵作为结构先验嵌入多层神经网络，构建项目-技能关系的结构化表示；2) 设计带L2惩罚的损失函数，对与Q矩阵不对齐的技能激活进行惩罚；3) 提出可解释对齐评估指标，量化预测技能激活与项目层面技能标签的匹配程度。

Result: M-QCDNet在保持认知理论一致性的前提下实现了良好的预测性能，提出的对齐评估指标能够有效衡量模型的结构可解释性，模型在课堂实践中可早期检测学习困难并支持掌握导向的干预策略。

Conclusion: M-QCDNet通过将诊断有效性嵌入模型设计，弥合了心理测量透明性与神经网络灵活性之间的鸿沟，推动了可解释、公平且可操作的认知诊断人工智能发展。该方法在课堂评估、学习困难早期检测和个性化干预等教育场景具有实用价值。

Abstract: The research proposes a multilayer Q-matrix-embedded neural network for cognitive diagnosis (M-QCDNet), which integrates the structural interpretability of cognitive diagnostic models (CDMs) with the deep learning neural network (NN). M-QCDNet structures the item-skill relationship using the Q-matrix as a structural prior, ensuring latent mastery profiles remain interpretable and consistent with cognitive theory, followed by the proposed loss function with an L2 penalty to penalize skills not aligned with the Q-matrix and to balance predictive performance and structural alignment. Corresponding evaluation matrices, the interpretable alignment-based metrics that quantify the degree to which predicted skill activations correspond to item-level skills, were further developed. M-QCDNet offers practical benefits for classroom practice, enabling early detection of learning difficulties and supporting mastery-based interventions. By embedding diagnostic validity into model design, M-QCDNet bridges psychometric transparency and neural flexibility, advancing interpretable, fair, and actionable AI for cognitive diagnostics.

</details>


### [324] [I\textsuperscript{2}RiMA: Spectral Riemannian Representation with Temporal Attention for Mental Stress Detection based on EEG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01279)
*Cheng He,Kunyu Peng,Shangen Han,Jinming Ma,Jinhong Ding,Likun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为IIRMFormer的脑电情绪应激检测方法，通过构建逐频点的空间协方差矩阵并映射到SPD切空间，结合频率聚类聚合和帧间注意力机制，在三个数据集上以1.60M参数和31.95M FLOPs实现最高82.78%的平衡准确率，优于五种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨被试脑电应激检测中，判别性应激模式既依赖被试个体差异又具有频率特异性。传统黎曼方法主要在时域建模空间协方差，忽略了高频认知状态解码所需的关键神经振荡信息；而标准时序tokenization往往破坏帧间时间连贯性。

Method: 1) 在每个频率点独立构建空间协方差矩阵并映射到SPD切空间，保留通道几何结构与频率特异性判别信息；2) 引入频率聚类聚合（frequency cluster aggregation）选择信息性光谱成分，形成与脑电节律对齐的紧凑数据驱动频率簇；3) 帧内-帧间注意力模块（intra-inter slice attention）自适应整合局部帧级光谱动态与全局时间上下文。

Result: 在三个数据集上一致优于五种SOTA基线，最高平衡准确率达82.78%，参数量仅1.60M，FLOPs仅31.95M。

Conclusion: 所提出的IIRMFormer通过逐频点黎曼流形建模与频率聚类聚合，有效解决了跨被试脑电应激检测中的个体差异和频率特异性问题，在效率和性能上均具优势。

Abstract: Cross-subject EEG stress detection remains challenging because discriminative stress-related patterns are both subject-dependent and frequency-specific. Conventional Riemannian methods model spatial covariance mainly in the time domain, overlooking neural oscillations that are critical for high-level cognitive state decoding, while standard temporal tokenization often fragments inter-slice temporal coherence. To address these limitations, we propose \method{}, an Intra-Inter Riemannian Manifold Attention Network for EEG-based stress detection. \method{} constructs spatial covariance matrices independently at each frequency point and maps them to the SPD tangent space, preserving channel-wise geometry together with frequency-specific discriminative cues. It further introduces frequency cluster aggregation to select informative spectral components and reduce redundancy by forming compact, data-driven frequency clusters aligned with EEG rhythms. Finally, an intra-inter slice attention module adaptively integrates local slice-level spectral dynamics and global temporal context across EEG sequences. Experiments on three datasets show that \method{} consistently outperforms five state-of-the-art baselines, achieving up to 82.78\% balanced accuracy while remaining efficient with only 1.60M parameters and 31.95M FLOPs.

</details>


### [325] [Fixed-Set Robustness in Programming by Example: Example Corruption and Semantic Partition Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01280)
*Yuan Si,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了编程示例系统在面对恶意选择错误示例时的对抗鲁棒性问题，提出了固定集最坏情况损坏的形式化方法、精确/启发式损坏搜索算法，以及基于版本空间分区聚合的防御策略。实验表明低裕度PBE任务存在对抗鲁棒性问题，而VPA防御仅在分区投票裕度足够时有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编程示例系统通常假设错误示例来自随机噪声过程，忽略了存在恶意攻击者故意选择破坏性示例的情况。本文旨在揭示这种对抗性故障模式，并探索相应的防御策略。

Method: (1) 形式化定义了有限PBE版本空间上的固定集最坏情况损坏；(2) 实现了精确（有界池内）和启发式的损坏搜索算法；(3) 提出版本空间分区聚合防御策略：在不相交的示例组上独立合成，然后通过语义签名进行投票聚合。

Result: (1) 一个精心设计的编辑即可使全部8个尖峰任务失败，而随机控制（200轮typo、DSL池、距离匹配随机）成功率仅10.3%/11.0%/16.7%；(2) 生成的低裕度（margin-1）示例在预算1下即可破坏，VPA可恢复；(3) 在公共SyGuS上投票裕度接近1，自适应攻击可使VPA准确率降至0；(4) Playgol在141个可接受任务上对随机控制有显著提升。

Conclusion: 低裕度PBE任务存在对抗鲁棒性维度，这是随机噪声评估所忽略的；语义分区聚合仅在干净语义保持分区投票裕度时有效，而这在现实任务中往往难以满足。

Abstract: Programming-by-example systems infer programs from a small set of input-output examples. Robust PBE work usually models wrong examples as samples from a stochastic noise process and then minimizes an expected or empirical loss. This paper studies a different failure mode: an adversary who sees the synthesizer and chooses the examples whose corruption most damages the returned program. We formalize fixed-set worst-case corruption for finite PBE version spaces, implement exact-within-bounded-pool and heuristic corruption searches for a string-transformation DSL, and introduce version-space partition aggregation (VPA), a defense that synthesizes on disjoint example groups and votes by semantic signatures. The central claim is deliberately bounded and partly negative: low-margin PBE tasks have an adversarial robustness dimension that random-typo and noisy-PBE evaluations miss, while semantic partition aggregation helps only when the clean semantics keep a partition vote margin, which often fails on realistic tasks. Evidence from curated/generated DSL tasks, accepted public SyGuS PBE_SLIA slices, SYNTRA Playgol v2, and noisy-PBE objective baselines supports that boundary. One curated edit flips all 8 spike tasks while 200-trial typo, DSL-pool, and distance-matched random controls succeed on 10.3%, 11.0%, and 16.7%; generated margin-1 rows flip under budget 1 yet VPA recovers them; on public SyGuS the vote margin is near one, so an adaptive attacker drives VPA accuracy to zero; accepted public SyGuS slices move across exact-within-pool budget boundaries; and Playgol shows positive paired-bootstrap gaps against typo and same-pool random controls on the 141 accepted rows. A small exact-output prompt harness over 20 controlled margin-1 tasks shows the same qualitative clean-to-attacked pattern across local and API models, while it is treated as a scope check, not a broad LLM benchmark.

</details>


### [326] [Domain Knowledge Based Temporal-Spatial Graph Convolution Network for ECG Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01282)
*Wenting Ma,Zhipeng Zhang,Xiaohang Yuan,Ningwei Xie,Yuxin Xie,Xiaolin Wang,Meng Guo,Xingang Chai,Zhenjie Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于领域知识的图卷积网络用于心电图识别，通过双流有向图建模心电周期内和周期间的空间与时间依赖关系，在中文心电竞赛数据集上取得了88.1%的平均F1分数，尤其对罕见类别提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI在医疗领域广泛部署，但模型可解释性不足仍是挑战。现有端到端CNN方法缺乏对心电图领域知识（如PRQST关键点）的显式利用，导致对罕见类别的识别性能不佳。

Method: 提出基于领域知识的双流有向图卷积网络：空间有向图建模同一心电周期内PRQST关键点之间的位置关系；时间有向图刻画相邻心电周期之间的时序依赖关系，从而同时捕获心电信号的内部结构特征与长期时序模式。

Result: 在首届中国心电智能竞赛九分类数据集上，模型整体平均F1分数达88.1%，罕见类别平均F1分数达76.3%，均优于现有最优模型。

Conclusion: 引入领域知识（PRQST关键点）结合图卷积网络能有效提升心电图识别性能，尤其对罕见类别的检测能力改善显著，验证了领域知识引导的图模型在医疗AI中的价值。

Abstract: In light of strides in Arti cial Intelligence (AI) and its wide spread application, challenges persist in the interpretability of AI models, particularly within specialized domains like healthcare, such as electro cardiograph (ECG) recognition. Rather than relying solely on end-to-end convolutional neural networks, this paper introduces a novel approach using a domain knowledge-based graph convolution network for ECG recognition. Key landmarks points of PRQST, vital to ECG interpreta tion, are incorporated as domain knowledge. The double-stream directed graph is employed to model both intra and inter ECG cycles.
